# PerpController local-fork probes

Investigation-only behavioral observations. No repository contract code was changed, and no transaction was broadcast beyond the local Anvil fork. The probe used public ABI data and black-box observations only; no target source or frontend bundles were read. The harness and all probe logs are under `/home/ubuntu/work/probes`.

## Fork and baseline

- Base chain ID: `8453`; fork block: `54990962`; Foundry test timestamp: `1794188568` (`2026-11-09T01:42:48Z`).
- Local fork endpoint: `http://127.0.0.1:8545`, forked from `https://mainnet.base.org`. Each Foundry test starts with fresh fork state.
- Controller: `0x682F3a053B0260daD4EbEee3924B1618B330A200`; owner/keeper Safe: `0xd3493F286f1B6e084D1CE5f6d6E6016fa7c8454d`.
- FeePolicy: `0x1E3DbDc2aDF4d5fDd1efDFB9FF05e4154D0c00E8`; Balancer: `0x30333a6B8744a1203D060E108dDC8C155059aCbf`; BondIssuer: `0xc56CCbF6219B780c879544FEe0404bD3B49fEBB0`; BondsManager: `0xe8c13955caED9c009fd2b335A10986407958fde3`.
- cbBTC: `0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf` (8 decimals); perpSr: `0x4be28E7597A928835245a10E9161E0c44687b70b`; perpJr: `0x8cd04668292dC32F519972495C179E4f02C0e8AB`.
- Initial `valueCap=1000000000`; `lastRebalanceTimestampSec=1794160800`; `systemDeviations=(1049678448816785329,1000925651512358813)`; `availPerpJrLiquidity=267684958`; `reservedPerpJrLiquidity=3864657`; `perpJrReservedUnderlyingAmt=1000000`.
- Initial perp supplies: SR `9581311974479`, JR `33850817003440`; reserve values: SR `129298890`, JR `386465740`.
- Initial latest bond ID `0x93dcba573477147233c33c344a994ff99eedbe3ac9c31c2c5198f72add177ca8`, `underlyingBal=82936982`, open price `75941640373000000000000`, maturity `1796324400`, SR ratio `500000000000000000`. Its tranches are SR `0x2b97045571Ec78aE1010Df47b3e275eCdAE53300` and JR `0x0BC26651B2Fbc4230F5C34a9eD186c28aEA48414`.
- No probe hit `ValueCapExceeded`; the cap was not raised.
- The full initial operation suite passed 16/16 tests. Later, both floor-focused tests passed (2/2), and the added lazy-flashMint test passed (1/1). These were targeted runs; a single monolithic suite was not rerun after the last two test additions.

## Operation findings

### `flashMint2`

- SR-only `1000000` sats returned `74102043524` perpSr and zero perpJr. The SR reserve underlying increased by `1000000`; JR underlying and JR tranche rows were unchanged.
- JR-only `1000000` sats returned `87590731854` perpJr. The JR reserve underlying increased by `1000000`.
- Both sides (`1000000` SR / `2000000` JR sats) returned `74094350215` perpSr and `175163276286` perpJr. `OpFees` were `7693309` perpSr and `18187423` perpJr; protocol fees were zero.
- 1000-sat inputs returned `74102043` perpSr and/or `87590731` perpJr as applicable; integer fee amounts rounded to zero.
- An SR-only mint did not allocate a JR tranche or change the JR reserve in the observed snapshots.

### `flashRedeem2`

- SR-only input `74102043524` perpSr returned `999999` cbBTC units (sats), with zero fee.
- JR-only input `87590731854` perpJr returned `998796` sats. `1203` cbBTC units were retained as collected fees; protocol fee was zero.
- Both-side input (`74094350215` perpSr / `175163276286` perpJr) returned `2997699` sats. The two cbBTC fee events were `663` and `1326` (total `1989`); protocol fees were zero.
- The reserve-floor attempt used a locally assigned perpJr balance equal to supply. Full-supply input `33850817003440` reverted with `0x08c379a0` (`ERC20: transfer amount exceeds balance`). A second attempt at the calculated available-liquidity boundary, `23446721377765` perpJr, reverted with controller error `OpDisabled()` (`0x07380a2e`). This selector is in the public Controller ABI. The boundary attempt is the relevant reserve-floor probe; the full-supply attempt is recorded separately as an overlarge input.

### `mint2` and `redeem2`

- Depositing `1000000` sats into the latest bond returned `37970820186` SR tranche units and `37970820187` JR tranche units. Latest-bond `underlyingBal` changed from `82936982` to `83936982`.
- `mint2` returned `37507090992` perpSr and `43035866540` perpJr. Fee percentages were `2515394810867535` per side; fee tokens collected were `94583055` perpSr and `108525179` perpJr, with zero protocol fee.
- `redeem2` returned SR underlying `0`, SR tranche amounts `[12427697636,12218662440,13228887052]`, JR underlying `344792`, and JR tranche amounts `[4046798160,3978730585,4307687341]`. The raw appendix includes each tranche address and every before/after token/reserve snapshot.

### Rebalance timing and reserve movement

- `rebalanceFrequencySec=86400`, `rebalanceWindowOffsetSec=64800`, and `rebalanceWindowLengthSec=86400`.
- Samples: `1794160799 -> [1794074400,1794160800]`; `1794160800`, `1794160801`, `1794247199 -> [1794160800,1794247200]`; `1794247200` and `1794247201 -> [1794247200,1794333600]`.
- The near-last attempt at `1794160799` was inside the preceding window and reverted `LastRebalanceTooRecent()` (`0xa3b3f090`). The interval length equals the frequency, so the sampled windows tile time; no timestamp outside a window was isolated.
- At `1794247200`, `rebalance()` succeeded and emitted `Rebalance(rebalanceValue=28296,isPerpSrPayer=false)`. SR underlying increased `0 -> 28296`; JR underlying decreased `271549615 -> 271521319`; tranche balances were unchanged. Immediate repeat reverted `LastRebalanceTooRecent()` (`0xa3b3f090`).

### `upkeep`, `rollover`, maturity, and lazy issuance

- Next issue time was `1794510000` (`2026-11-12T19:00:00Z`); nearest initial reserve maturity was `926232` seconds away, at `1795114800` (`2026-11-19T19:00:00Z`).
- Warped-time tests locally mocked the Chainlink feed (`0x07DA0E54543a844a80ABE69c8A12F22B3aA59f9D`) with the observed answer `7482940645796` and a fresh timestamp, and locally marked the sequencer feed (`0xBCF85224fc0756B9Fa45aA7892530B47e10b6433`) up past its 3600-second grace period before calling `oracle.cachePrice()`. Without this local-only adjustment, the stale feed caused `InvalidPrice()` after warps. This is a harness accommodation, not a natural feed update.
- At `1794510001`, explicit `upkeep()` succeeded, emitted a `BondMature` with zero claims for `0x72b690a18bb8c7b31702dbfa82d4ad74ca207bd7e71cd58992753279b4e4b22`, and created latest bond `0xff73192993e9aaaf7db24d86d5156c9c9eac5bfeadff2a6ffc2fd3bd8e74181b` (maturity `1796929200`). New reserve tranche entries initially had zero balance/value.
- `rollover()` then emitted `TranchesRedeemed` for bond `0x845535d5c774b4204fe67ddcdd33292c39defdaa7092f1d562bdb50a3b102963` (`srAmtRedeemed=3133547705520`, `jrAmtRedeemed=3133547705527`, `underlyingAmtReturned=77232982`). It emitted deposits of `6411258` into bond `0x93dc...` and `77245228` into the new `0xff7319...` bond. The old tranche rows disappeared from the reserve arrays; latest-bond `underlyingBal` became `77245228`, while JR reserve underlying changed `271549615 -> 265126111`.
- After warping to `1795436234`, `upkeep()` emitted `BondMature` for `0x845535...` (zero claims, close price `74829406457960000000000`) and created bond `0x5a83ee1f5b5b29d9c5adfc39fbd15a9b3d5aec0d793fc824a97b3bed97d9fd50` (maturity `1797534000`). Subsequent `rollover()` redeemed bond `0x342b85e9d38918a8718c6ea642ea0df75e75c938bc45a6efde8c1dbaa3ef108d` (`srAmtRedeemed=3392625737181`, `jrAmtRedeemed=3392625737183`, `underlyingAmtReturned=84045051`) and deposited into bonds `0xff7319...` and `0x5a83...`. Reserve snapshots show old tranche rows removed and the latest bond `underlyingBal=77245228`.
- A separate fresh-fork test called `flashMint2` directly at `1795114801`, after both the next issue time and nearest maturity, without first calling `upkeep()` or `rollover()`. It minted `74102043` perpSr for 1000 sats, but emitted no `BondCreated`, `BondMature`, or rollover events; latest bond ID and `underlyingBal` remained unchanged. No lazy issuance/rollover was observed on that path.
- `minCleanupVal` was `1000000` throughout; the tests did not vary it independently. Raw reserve arrays show zero-value tranche entries after `upkeep` and old rows removed after `rollover`, but the exact cleanup threshold was not isolated.

### Pause controls

- Owner Safe impersonation successfully called `pauseUserOps(true/false)` and `pauseSystemOps(true/false)`.
- While user operations were paused, `flashMint2`, `flashRedeem2`, `mint2`, and `redeem2` reverted with `UserOpsPaused()` (`0xb3d197c4`).
- While system operations were paused, `rebalance`, `upkeep`, and `rollover` reverted with `SystemOpsPaused()` (`0x237af6f0`).

## Limits and unresolved observations

- No true outside-window timestamp was tested: with `windowLength == frequency`, every timestamp is in a window. The attempted near-last timestamp was explicitly logged as inside its preceding window.
- The full-supply JR redeem reverted with `ERC20: transfer amount exceeds balance`; the failing ERC20/callsite was not isolated. The separately computed available-liquidity-boundary attempt returned `OpDisabled()`. The harness uses `deal`-style local balance assignment, not a verified historical holder.
- A perp-token event with topic `0xd1fbbd81a5fd46d869b5cd6883d99108cd295072be1b09d36381bbc78b686939` has raw address/amount data, but no supplied public ABI identifies its event name. It is retained as `UNKNOWN` with raw topics/data.
- `cast logs` requests for holder discovery hit Base RPC HTTP 429. No retry was needed for the Foundry fork tests.
- All observations are integer values. cbBTC amounts are raw 8-decimal units; WAD-style values are not rescaled in the raw appendix.

## Derived formulas (hypotheses)

- **Flash mint conversion — exact for tested inputs.** `floor(underlyingIn * preSupply / preReserveValue)` matches one-sided 1000000-sat SR (`74102043524`) and JR (`87590731854`) outputs, and each 1000-sat output (`74102043` SR, `87590731` JR). For the both-side input, gross SR/JR outputs are `74102043524` / `175181463709`; subtracting `OpFees` `7693309` / `18187423` gives the observed `74094350215` / `175163276286` exactly.
- **Flash redeem conversion — exact for tested inputs.** `floor(perpIn * preReserveValue / preSupply)` gives gross outputs: SR-only `999999`; JR-only `999999`; and both-side SR/JR `999896` / `1999792`. Subtracting the corresponding cbBTC fees (`0`, `1203`, or `663`/`1326`) matches returned outputs exactly (`999999`, `998796`, and combined `2997699`).
- **`mint2` tranche valuation and perps — exact under per-tranche floors.** For the deposited tranche amounts, `floor(trancheAmount * trancheValue / trancheSupply)` gives SR value `507431` and JR value `492568` (sum `999999`, one unit below the `1000000`-sat deposit due to independent floors). Using those inputs and the pre-mint reserve values/supplies, `floor(valueIn * perpSupply / reserveValue)` gives gross perp outputs `37601674047` SR and `43144391719` JR, exactly equal to returned perps plus their `OpFees` (`37507090992+94583055` and `43035866540+108525179`). Reserve claim deltas were `507432` SR plus `492568` JR, totaling the original `1000000`; the SR aggregate value delta differs from the independently floored input value by one unit.
- **SR-only flash-mint junior allocation — no allocation observed.** After an SR-only 1000000-sat mint, JR underlying, JR supply, and all JR tranche rows were unchanged; the full input accrued to SR underlying. This is an exact observation for that case, not a claim about all configurations.
- **Fee token accounting — exact in sampled calls.** `collectedFeeBal` equaled the Controller’s balance of the fee token and the emitted `OpFees.totalFeeAmt`; `protocolFeeBals` and `protocolFeeAmt` were zero in the tested mint/redeem flows. Flash-redeem fees were collected in cbBTC; mint fees were collected in the corresponding perp token.
- **JR reserve boundary — observed selector, internal meaning not proven.** The calculated candidate `23446721377765` perpJr (one unit above `floor(supply * availPerpJrLiquidity / reserveValue)`) reverted with `OpDisabled()` (`0x07380a2e`). This is the observed boundary result; no target source was used to infer the internal guard.
